:robot: I have created a release *beep* *boop*
---


## [6.10.0](https://github.com/morluto/rea/compare/rea-agents-6.9.0...rea-agents-6.10.0) (2026-11-19)


### ⚠ BREAKING CHANGES

* **debuggers:** preserve exact Cutter output and GNU MI escapes ([#12457](https://github.com/morluto/rea/issues/12457))
* **ghidra:** A DOS COM file that ComLoader accepts now loads at linear 0x100 instead of 0x10100. Read load_address. The BinaryLoader fallback remains at 1000:0100.
* **ghidra:** Ghidra 12.1.x is rejected. Use Ghidra 12.2 or another 12.x build at or above minor 2, and a JDK inside that installation's declared range (25 or newer for the verified 12.2 build).
* **cli:** Markdown argument diagnostics use REA's literal escaping, table widths and token counts. Flag-shaped unknown MCP subcommands honor the selected format. Completion --schema/--version routes use the canonical REA-owned hook rather than the retired SDK generator. Refresh affected literal snapshots and regenerate completion hooks with rea completions <shell>.
* **cli:** rea <command> --mcp is removed. Start REA's MCP server with rea mcp or rea --mcp.
* **mcp:** MCP calls to inspect_pcap_capture, start_rizin_debug_session and start_gdb_session with a relative path or inferior_tty are refused with invalid_request. Pass absolute paths.
* **frida:** MCP `load_frida_script` and `instrument_with_frida` refuse a relative script `path` with `invalid_request`; send an absolute path. CLI `--script-file` still accepts a relative path, and Evidence `source_path` is now the resolved absolute path.
* **android:** Android JNI Evidence whose library failure has kind "integrity" must name integrity_check. Records written before #11191 that lack it no longer parse; recreate them.
* **ida:** "protocol": "legacy" IDA registrations are refused. Before (accepted, ida-pro-mcp 1.4 GUI proxy):   {"mode": "attached", "protocol": "legacy", "command": "python",    "args": ["/path/ida_pro_mcp/server.py", "--ida-rpc", "http://127.0.0.1:13337"]} After (native plugin for an open GUI database):   {"mode": "attached", "protocol": "native", "url": "http://127.0.0.1:13337/mcp"} or an ida-pro-mcp 2.x database supervisor in headless mode:   {"mode": "headless", "command": "idalib-mcp", "args": ["--stdio", "--max-workers", "1"]} procedure_callers is no longer available from any IDA profile, and IDA procedure_assembly/read_function_instructions no longer advertise may_show_ui. See docs/providers/ida-provider.md#migrate-a-legacy-attached-registration.
* process capture Evidence IDs change: captures without PID normalization name `{ kind: "process", pid, start_time, executable_sha256 }` as their subject, and captures with PID normalization name `{ kind: "unknown", reason: "withheld-by-caller" }`. Capture results require `manifest.root_process`; captures without it are refused. Recreate stored process capture Evidence.
* **evidence:** inline inventory Evidence for an ESP image root without `format_metadata.flash_dump`, and PE certificate Evidence whose signers lack `unsigned_attribute_oids`, are refused as invalid input. Recreate the observation with the current REA.
* **snapshots:** analysis snapshot files change shape: `target` is `{ kind, identity: { kind: "file", sha256, format, architecture, selection } }` and entry `execution.subject` is the Evidence subject (`{ identity, name, local_path }`, never null). Snapshot binding mismatch details report `target.snapshot`/`target.selected` in the same shape. Evidence IDs of universal Mach-O targets change because their subject identity now carries the selected slice. Recreate saved snapshots and Evidence bundles of universal Mach-O targets.
* **evidence:** Evidence format revision 2. Evidence IDs commit to the subject's target identity (subject.identity, including the raw/DOL entry point or ESP flash offsets), and every record declares evidence_format: 2. Evidence bundles, records and analysis snapshots from earlier releases are refused; recreate the observations with this release.
* **native:** inspect_native_initializers `coverage.status` reports `partial` or `unknown` instead of `incomplete`. Treat both as not complete; `partial` means declared slots or names were left unreported, `unknown` means the report could not establish completeness.
* **managed:** compare_managed_members item limitations are objects, not strings, and limitation_reasons is removed. Read limitations[].code (and side) instead of matching message prefixes such as "signature-not-resolved:". Comparison Evidence written by earlier builds is refused; recreate it.
* **providers:** Ghidra Evidence `analysis_profile.parameters` replace the semantic marker keys and `bridge_sources_sha256` with `producer` ({rea_version, sources_sha256}), and selection values lose their "-vN" suffix (for example `language_selection: "provider-pinned"`). Ghidra Evidence IDs and profile digests change, and analysis snapshots saved by earlier builds are refused at open. Migration: open the target without the old snapshot and save a fresh one; consumers reading the removed marker keys should read `producer` instead.
* **model:** inspect_model_artifact `coverage.array_values` reports `partial` where it reported `preview`; `complete` is unchanged.
* **native:** inspect_kernel_build_config `coverage.status` reports `partial` (config_gzip_truncated, config_search_exhausted) or `unknown` (malformed config, payload not searched) instead of `incomplete`.
* **native:** inspect_elf_unwind_ranges `coverage.status` reports `partial` where it reported `incomplete`. Callers comparing against `incomplete` must compare against `partial`; `complete` is unchanged.
* **cli:** MCP failures no longer carry details.limit.cli_flag, and native deadline remediation text no longer names the CLI flag. CLI output still reports cli_flag for the command that ran.
* **evidence:** inspect_firmware_regions and extract_firmware results require `analysis_timeout_ms`, and inspect_managed_members call edges and field accesses require `target_row_resolved`. Evidence or analysis-view input without them is refused. Every producer in this release writes them; recreate observations made by a build that omitted them.
* **browser:** browser network requests without redirects are refused as malformed; recreate the capture.
* **native:** `rea inspect-managed-members --evidence-bundle <path>` is now `--evidence_output <path>`; the old flag is refused as an unknown flag. With `--detail summary`, stdout is now `{"summary": <summary Evidence>, "evidence_bundle": {"path", "bytes"}}` instead of the summary Evidence alone, and a publication failure is the shared delivery's error projection without the `error` label or `input_path`. A summary without a destination names `evidence_output` in its issue path. Migrate by renaming the flag and reading `.summary`. Compatibility is not kept: one spelling cannot mean "read this bundle" on view and workflow commands and "write this bundle" on one producer.
* **mcp:** `REA_MCP_INPUT_SCHEMA_PROFILE=full` and `REA_MCP_OUTPUT_SCHEMA_PROFILE=full` are refused at startup and by `rea setup` with a configuration error naming the setting. Remove the setting from the client registration (or rerun setup with `REA_MCP_INPUT_SCHEMA_PROFILE=compact` / `REA_MCP_OUTPUT_SCHEMA_PROFILE=compact`). `REA_MCP_INPUT_SCHEMA_PROFILE` still accepts `compact`. Server-side validation is unchanged.
* **evidence:** JavaScript runtime observations without scripts.parse_failed_total, and active Electron Evidence without parameters.resolved_paths, are refused as malformed; recreate the observation.
* **javascript:** managed records missing these fields are refused as malformed; recreate the observation.
* **process:** managed records missing these fields are refused as malformed; recreate the observation.
* **managed:** managed records missing these fields are refused as malformed; recreate the observation.
* **evidence:** Evidence bundles written by REA 6.x (no `evidence_format`) are refused by import, comparison, reconstruction and analysis-snapshot readers as an integrity mismatch telling the caller to recreate the observations; re-exporting an earlier bundle does not update its format or identities. Bundles constructed by hand must include `"evidence_format": 1`. 6.7-era artifact summaries are refused by compare_artifacts, and SQLite and Dart AOT Evidence without the newly required fields fails validation.
* **ida:** an attached IDA registration without `protocol` is refused with a configuration error naming the choice. Add `"protocol": "legacy"` to an ida-pro-mcp 1.4 proxy registration (or `"protocol": "native"` for the ida-free-mcp plugin). Headless registrations are unchanged.
* **cutter:** cutter_command's result fields output_truncated and document_generation are now nullable. A caller that read document_generation as a number must handle null by listing Cutter sessions again before choosing an expected_generation. A caller that read output_truncated as a boolean must treat null as "the output may be incomplete".
* **adb:** preserve malformed version declarations
* **process:** a quiesced settlement may now report `cleanup_outcome: "cleaned"`. Saved captures stay valid; readers that accepted only `not_required` for quiesced settlements must accept `cleaned`.
